Seen in the pre-fix bench (2 rounds in `ceV`: Majesty played, 2 blood stolen anyway). *(2b) MY OWN STEAL TAKES WHAT THEY HOLD* (found verifying (2); a rules correction, unconditional): pairing each stealer row with its victim's row (not the series totals, which had been explained as capping) showed the stealer gaining the card's full number from a vampire holding 0 or 1 — about one steal round in five, in every arm, pre-fix control included: blood created from nothing since v0.6.19. The rulebook's Steal Blood "moves blood counters ... from the target to the striking minion" (quoted in the VEKN thread *Anathema, zero blood vampire and steal blood strike*). `_stealAvail()` reads the opposing vampire's blood from the board model; the gain, the round's `stole` and the strike choice use `min(card, their blood)` — against an empty vampire the steal is kept and the fist is the strike (1 damage they cannot mend = torpor). Unknown blood (no crypt record: a human table before any scan, an ally) keeps the card's number. The one-line steal shape still says the card's number (`strikes: steals 2 blood.`) — the victim moves what it has, a bot by `min(n, blood)`, a human by the panel. *(3) THE DAMAGE STEP* (Johan asked what is best): when nothing reaches the human's minion (their dodge, my steal, long-range fists) there is nothing to prevent — no wait, in every tempo, Turn-based included; when damage is coming, Standard waits 1 × the slider (was 2 ×; Adaptive's ceiling likewise); no botcfg unchanged. *Tests:* suite **1838/0** (sections 129 combat ends, 130 the damage step, 131 the steal, 132 my steal against 0 / 1 / unknown / 5 blood — 132a–b fail on the pre-cap bytes; 97d pins `combatEndStats: 0` — it tests the play's mechanics, not the decision; 128b's damage figure 2000 → 1000; the 131 battery fails on v0.6.195: blood 2 at the decision, 1 after Majesty), canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0, test-cardfx 261/0. *Verified at the bot table* (mechanism series, no variant, seeds 9101–9112, `REPORT-bench-controls.md`): `fix` (before the stealer cap) — 0 rounds where a vampire ended the combat or dodged and still lost blood to a steal, but 8 of 42 steal pairs where the stealer gained more than an empty victim lost; `fix2` (final) — steal pairs 34 of 34 equal (59 = 59), handshake 490 of 491, invariant flags at the control's level on the same seeds (32 vs 31), one timeout (a 38-turn endgame, not stuck). *Tools:* `ana-ledger.py` pairs each steal row with its victim's row (`4 steal pairs`).
- **v0.6.195 (9 Oct 2026)** — main's reply to v0.6.193 (client v2.6.139 / srv-2.6.43, `REPLY-to-bot-2026-10-09-v0.6.193.md`) reviewed against v0.6.194. *Their tempo wire* matches the parser exactly (whole state from the server, partial client writes, the stored tempo replayed after `joined`, the panel's `typeof bot._handlers.botcfg === 'function'` check, their `NAME set bot tempo: …` log line types as `other` and moves nothing) — no change needed there. *(1) THE HANDSHAKE AFTER A STEP — found by timing a whole combat round, not by reading the wait I had changed:* the combat steps waited on the tempo, but the handshake waits that FOLLOW them (`_awaitOppPress` after the press step, `_awaitOppStrike` via `_strikeWaitMs`, Taste of Vitae's `_awaitOppTaken`) still armed a full `askSecs` — 20 s at the client's spawn — after the human had already answered. "No" to "Any presses?" (every combat) and "No" to "What is your strike?" each bought 20 s of dead air; a round against a human who answers at once took 7.7 s at askSecs 3 and now takes 3.0 s. `_lineGraceMs(legacy, kind)`: vs a HUMAN under a tempo the handshake only catches a line already on its way — 0.3 s after No / the button / a skip, 1 s after a clock or a `ph` step, 1.5 s for the taken line their client writes as `ph` steps past Damage; `_phaseLast` records how the step ended. *(2) THE PRESS TO END:* those silent `askSecs` were also the only window a human had to cancel my press when they acted first and said "No" — shortening them alone would have taken a legal option away. Now, under a tempo, after my press to continue the bot asks "Any presses?" again (`_phaseStep(5, 'reply')`, on the tempo, never skipped); their press to end cancels my press as before. *(3) `res: 1` on the combat close* (main's offer): the client's own Combat-Resolve convention — every human's Played batch closes and their own clear setting applies; a bare `null` left it open until their next action because a blocked action returns from combat without "It resolves". The block close stays bare (the client's Block resolve never sends `res`), the vote close too (my referendum ends with "It resolves" either way). Not touched, on purpose: `_writeTally({ mode: null })` leaves the local mirror on the old mode until the echo — `_onVoteClose()` depends on that (`wasVote`). *Bot-vs-bot and a table with no botcfg are unchanged* (the grace and the reply window need a tempo AND a human opponent). *Tools:* `twd-profile.py` / `twd-archetypes.py` write a `.js` sibling from the same serialize (`globalThis.ELYSIUM_TWD_PROFILE` / `ELYSIUM_TWD_ARCHETYPES`, the cardfx compiler's file:// vehicle) — main's ask; both rebuilt here and the JSONs are byte-identical to the benched files. *START-HERE-BOTS.html:* the deck reading (TWDA), the four files + their `.js` twins, the tempo as main built it (the gear on the 🤖 badge, the ▶ beside it, seeded Standard 3 s, any seated player), the panel online (every seated player sees it, steps act in the host's tab), Close step / Close poll, the footer. *Gates:* suite **1812/0** (128i–j, 127q's res), canary 57/0 vs v2.6.138, hotseat 10/0, test-cardfx 261/0.
- **v0.6.194 (9 Oct 2026)** — THE TEMPO (Johan's design of 27 Sep, `spec-bot-tempo-2026-09-27.md`; the players' complaint: the wait against bots). *The numbers behind the complaint:* the client spawns bots with `askSecs: 20`, and a combat step waited `max(6, askSecs × 3)` = **60 s** for a human's answer. *Built:* one tempo object (`this.tempo`, `_waitPlan(kind, who)`) replaces the fixed clocks in every wait on a HUMAN — the block window and the reactions-only window (`_askBlock` / `_restartAskTimer`), the pool question (`_pqArm`), the vote clock (`_refPoll`), the seven combat steps (`_phaseStep`) and the damage step (`_phaseDamage`). The table sets it with `botcfg` ({ tempo: standard|adaptive|turn, waitSecs: 1–8 }, partial writes keep the other field, the first partial write fills the missing one with the spec's default Standard / 3.0 s; a change re-arms an OPEN wait at once). *Modes:* STANDARD — the slider is the clock, a combat step twice it (3 s → 6 s instead of 60). ADAPTIVE — the slider is the ceiling; the bot keeps each human's last 7 answer times (`_rtNote`, a running median) and waits 1.5 × the median, never under 0.8 s, never over the ceiling of that kind; and it SKIPS a question the human cannot act on — no block question when the seat has no ready minion, or only locked minions and an empty hand (a wake needs a card; the spec's "no unlocked minion" was read strictly so a wake-block is never taken from a human); no reactions-only question with an empty hand; in combat no "anything before …" question with an empty hand, no maneuver / damage wait with an empty hand and no equipment on the minion; the strike and the press are ALWAYS asked (the press is a decision, not a capability). TURN-BASED — no clock: the question says "(no clock — I wait for you)", `status()` reports no deadline (the widget shows its button), the Bot button (`step()`) gives the bot the word — new rungs `resolve-phase` (a combat step) and `resolve-vote` (my polls) beside `resolve-ask` / `resolve-pq`; a human's Pass during a contested block declares the verdict at once; the room's reaction stopwatch is never rung (its ring would re-stamp a deadline); safety net: a chat reminder after `tempoRemindMs` (120 s), the wait resolves as the clock would after `tempoGiveUpMs` (240 s), with a note. *No botcfg = exactly today* (the legacy plan: askSecs + chat settle, phaseSecs). *Bot-vs-bot untouched in every mode:* the plan is read only when a human is among the seats waited on (`mode: 'bots'` otherwise = askSecs). *Observability:* a `wait` trace row per human wait (kind, mode, who, ms, end = answer / clock / button / skip) — also on the process log, since a human table writes no trace; `ana-wait.py` sums them (seconds waited by kind, how the waits ended). *CLI:* `--tempo standard:3|adaptive:3|turn` on `elysium-bot.js` and `elysium-bot-table.js` (v1.2.0) for a table whose client sends no botcfg yet. *Gates:* suite **1803/0** (section 128, 54 assertions: botcfg, the three modes, the skips, the safety net, the button, mid-combat botcfg, the wait rows; the battery aborts on v0.6.193), canary 57/0 vs client v2.6.138, hotseat 10/0 (bot-only: the tempo never applies). *Not in this release:* the two short graces (`whoBlocksGraceMs` 2.5 s, `modGraceMs` 4 s) keep their fixed values in every mode; the bots' own handshake windows (`_strikeWaitMs`, `_awaitOpp*`) are unchanged by design.
- **v0.6.193 (28 Sep 2026)** — `dynFloor` defaults to 1. The re-bench (arm `dyn2`, 48 matches, the variant in every seat, the floor PROVEN to fire: 684 raises in 23 of 48 matches) came out −0.12 ± 0.39 VP, 11 wins of 48, against the negative control's +0.14 ± 0.45 and 12 — inside the noise, no loss shown. Both knowledge bricks are on by default again, this time on a real bench. Noted for a follow-up arm: the mean leans below the control and the floor fires often; counting only Creeping Sabotage and Fame (Smiling Jack drains every seat alike) may be the better rule. Suite / canary / hotseat on the default flip.
- **v0.6.192 (28 Sep 2026)** — ARCHETYPE LABELS, explanation only (knob `arkChat`, default 1 = one chat line per seat; labels never drive a decision). *Data:* `twd-archetypes.py` joins vtes-skill's reviewed classification (120 named archetypes, 2021+) to KRCG's TWDA by deck id — 42 archetypes with ≥ 10 decks (690, 15 % of the TWDA) — and writes `elysium-twd-archetypes.json` (55 KB): a naive-Bayes signature per archetype (card counts over its decks, clan counts). *Offline test (`REPORT-archetypes-offline.md`):* 5-fold, k shown cards + clan — inside the classified world 90 % right at 8 cards (99 % top-3); in the OPEN world (85 % of decks belong to no archetype) a plain margin is falsely confident on 38–54 %, a background test does not help, a COVERAGE gate does: label only with margin ≥ 2 AND ≥ 85 % of the shown cards typical of the archetype (in ≥ 20 % of its decks) → 49 % of classifiable decks labelled at 8 cards, 98 % right, ~1 in 10 outside decks mislabelled. *Bot:* `_archetype(seat)` (best, second, margin, coverage, tier confident / closest) from the seat's clan and shown library cards; a confident label is said once per seat ("I read Johan's deck as Lawfirm (9 of 9 cards typical).") and traced (`archetype` row); a closest read is traced only. Also: the TWDA profile rebuilt from 4,672 decks (the VDB export Johan found holds the same decks plus 134 newer ones, 82 from 2026, merged by date + player + name). Tests 127z (5). Suite 1749/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.192 (28 Sep 2026)** — ARCHETYPE LABELS, explanation only (knob `arkChat`, default 1 = one chat line per seat; labels never drive a decision). *Data:* `twd-archetypes.py` joins vtes-skill's reviewed classification (120 named archetypes, 2021+) to KRCG's TWDA by deck id — 42 archetypes with ≥ 10 decks (690, 15 % of the TWDA) — and writes `elysium-twd-archetypes.json` (55 KB): a naive-Bayes signature per archetype (card counts over its decks, clan counts). *Offline test (`REPORT-archetypes-offline.md`):* 5-fold, k shown cards + clan — inside the classified world 90 % right at 8 cards (99 % top-3); in the OPEN world (85 % of decks belong to no archetype) a plain margin is falsely confident on 38–54 %, a background test does not help, a COVERAGE gate does: label only with margin ≥ 2 AND ≥ 85 % of the shown cards typical of the archetype (in ≥ 20 % of its decks) → 49 % of classifiable decks labelled at 8 cards, 98 % right, ~1 in 10 outside decks mislabelled. *Bot:* `_archetype(seat)` (best, second, margin, coverage, tier confident / closest) from the seat's clan and shown library cards; a confident label is said once per seat ("I read Johan's deck as Lawfirm (9 of 9 cards typical).") and traced (`archetype` row); a closest read is traced only. Also: the TWDA profile rebuilt from 4,672 decks (the VDB export Johan found holds the same decks plus 134 newer ones, 82 from 2026, merged by date + player + name). Tests 127z (5). Suite 1749/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.191 (27 Sep 2026)** — the pre-handover review: brick 2 was INERT in real play. `_knownLosses` counted only my own pool lines, and the Creeping Sabotage tick is the PREDATOR's line ('Creeping Sabotage ticks — <prey> burns N', or the frozen 'Creeping Sabotage (xN): <prey> burns N pool.') naming me as target — never my own. It now counts any creep / Jack / Fame loss about me (target = my seat, or my own line). Consequence: the v0.6.190 bench (arm `dyn`) measured nothing, so `dynFloor` goes back to 0 until the re-bench (arm `dyn2`, running). Test 127y now feeds the predator's line. **Lesson (CLAUDE.md, the bench gate):** a bench cannot tell a harmless change from an inert one — verify the knob FIRES before reading its arm. Suite 1744/0, canary 57/0.
- **v0.6.190 (27 Sep 2026)** — `dynFloor` defaults to 1. Bench arm `dyn` (48 matches, the variant in every seat): +0.17 ± 0.44 VP, 13 wins of 48, against the negative control's +0.14 ± 0.45 and 12 — level, no loss; the raised floor acts only in the few turns a bot carries Creeping Sabotage, Smiling Jack or Fame. Both knowledge bricks are now on by default (`twdKnow`, `dynFloor`). The Tremere-pinned arms are in the report too: without knowledge the Tremere starter wins 1 of 24 (−0.62 ± 0.30) — a deck-balance fact; with the sniper's pacifist press 3 of 24 (−0.46 ± 0.43), inside the noise, kept on. `REPORT-bench-controls.md` carries all seven arms. Suite / canary / hotseat on the default flip.
- **v0.6.189 (27 Sep 2026)** — THE KNOWLEDGE LAYER, brick 2: "do not oust yourself" (knob `dynFloor`, default 0 until the bench; arena v1.4.5 whitelists it). The other bot project's players saw a bot influence down to 1 pool with a Creeping Sabotage on it; ours already raises its critical pool with the predator's bleed burst (v0.6.136) but not with the RECURRING losses that come whatever the predator does. `_knownLosses()` reads from the event log what I paid to Creeping Sabotage ticks, Smiling Jack and Fame over my last full turn cycle (between my last two turn starts) and, with dynFloor 1, adds it to the burst in `_critPool` — the influence floor rises before the next tick. The event log now exists from a bot's birth (it was created at the first line). Tests 127y (2). Suite 1744/0, canary 57/0. Bench arms pending: `dynFloor` (48, every seat) and the Tremere-pinned `twdT` (24) still running.
- **v0.6.188 (27 Sep 2026)** — `twdKnow` defaults to 1. The mirror bench's arm `twd` (knob twdKnow=1 on the variant seat, 48 matches, the variant in every seat) came out **+0.21 ± 0.46 VP** against the negative control's +0.14 ± 0.45: no loss, no measurable gain at this size — expected, since the only decision change (the sniper's pacifist press) fires only when the variant is Tremere, and the combat-prior change only shifts a prior. `bench.sh` gains two optional arguments: a DECK name pins the variant to the seat holding that deck (measures a deck-specific rule), and a BASE knob sets every seat's baseline (needed once a knob's default has flipped). A deck-pinned arm `twdT` (Tremere always the variant, baseline seats twdKnow=0, 24 matches) is running to measure the sniper rule itself. `REPORT-bench-controls.md` updated (the `twd` arm, the two arguments). Suite 1742/0, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0 (default flip only).
- **v0.6.187 (27 Sep 2026)** — THE KNOWLEDGE LAYER, brick 1 (knob `twdKnow`, default 0 until the bench says otherwise; arena v1.4.4 whitelists it). *Data:* `twd-profile.py` reads KRCG's TWDA export (`twda.json`, 4,538 decks, 2015+ weighted double) and cardfx, and writes `elysium-twd-profile.json` (91 KB): per library card held by ≥ 6 decks (1,511 cards) the mean shares of a deck that holds it — combat (Combat cards, rushes, strikes, maneuvers, prevents), stealth (modifiers with stealth), intercept (reactions / equipment with intercept), bounce — and per clan (38) the same over decks led by that clan. Sanity: Bum's Rush 0.54 combat, Immortal Grapple 0.50, Lost in Crowds 0.23 stealth, Eyes of Argus 0.20 intercept; Brujah 0.37 combat, Malkavian 0.23 stealth, Ventrue the highest bounce. *Bot:* `_twdExpect(seat)` = the clan prior (from the seat's crypt) + every library card the seat has shown (the observation model AND the event log, capped at 2 per card), public evidence only; with `twdKnow` 1 it shapes the combat prior in `oppCombat` (weight = the evidence, then observations take over as before), and a Tremere sniper with a pacifist opponent (TWDA combat danger < 0.2 over ≥ 2 pieces of evidence, a press in hand) stays close for Theft + press + Theft instead of maneuvering — Johan's deferred question, the guide's rule. The profile is parsed once per process (a per-instance parse ran the suite's heap out). Tests 127x (5). Suite 1742/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0. **Bench:** arm `twd` (knob twdKnow=1 on the variant seat, 48 matches) against the negative control — see `REPORT-bench-controls.md` for the result once complete.
- **v0.6.186 (27 Sep 2026)** — the knowledge layer's feed, reviewed before the layer is built. Cards shown per seat (`ev.cardsShown`) now come from THREE sources: the `plays X` lines; the client's play clones (`fx`, deduped against a line for the same seat and card within 3 s — a human's play that never produced a line still counts); and the equip / employ / recruit lines (`uEquip`, the bot's `recruits X [disc].`). Discards are kept per seat as well (`ev.cardsDiscarded`) — a discard shows a card of the deck. Found in the review: the bot already keeps a per-opponent observation model (`this.opp[seat]`: plays counted from the fx clones AND from the ash-heap diff that never misses a play, effect categories seen, the public board — vampires, clans, disciplines, equipment); the knowledge layer builds on it for the board and categories, and on the event log for sequence and timing. Tests 127u +4. Suite 1737/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **Tools (27 Sep 2026, bot unchanged at v0.6.185)** — THE MIRROR BENCH (design Part 5). `bench.sh ARM WORKER NWORKERS N SPEC`: resumable (a finished match is skipped, so an interrupted run continues where it stopped); match I uses design line ((I−1)%12)+1, the variant seat rotates block by block through all four seats, seed 9100+I is shared by every arm; SPEC = `none` / `persona=novice` / `knob=key=val`. `ana-bench.py`: the variant seat's VP minus a quarter of the table's VP per match, mean, 95 % interval, wins. **Controls:** A vs A (48) a dead heat inside about ±0.5 VP; a crippled seat (`poolFloor=22`, 12) −0.81, interval wholly below 0, 0 wins — the bench sees a real handicap; the novice persona (16) not measurably weaker than expert — personas move behaviour more than strength. Lesson: the negative control leaned +0.5 while the variant had sat only in seats 1–2 — never read a bench before every seat is covered. `REPORT-bench-controls.md` (generated by `make-bench-report.sh`). CLAUDE.md: the bench gate.
- **v0.6.185 (26 Sep 2026)** — the phase-B review. A pending mark (strike, press, maneuver, taken) now takes only the SPEAKER's own unmarked event; the old second lookup — any speaker's unmarked event of that kind — could have picked up a bystander's line that the name filter had rejected (it stays unmarked in the log), and handed another combat's strike to mine. When the speaker's event is missing (a direct parser call), the parser's own values are recorded instead. Reviewed and left as is: the stale-pending resets at a new combat (all four kinds dropped), `_oppLastTaken` / `_oppCarrionCrows` keeping the old time-window and reset semantics, the combat episode's lifetime, the O(ring) scans (microseconds; cacheable later). Test 127w (2). Suite 1733/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0; bot-only series pb6 read with the ledger / combat / invariant tools.
- **v0.6.184 (26 Sep 2026)** — PHASE B, step 4 (the last): the opponent's TAKEN line and every-round damage live in the event log. `opp-taken` events carry the parser's semantics (`n` = blood they lost, `dmg` = damage taken, `now`, `down`): `_oppTaken` is the first pending one (Taste of Vitae's waiter consumes it), `_oppLastTaken` the latest one consumed or not (the press decision and the wounded-ends-combat rule read it). `opp-env` events hold the opponent's Carrion Crows / every-round damage; `_oppCarrionCrows` is the latest since the last reset. **With steps 1–4 the whole combat inflow — strike, press, maneuver, taken, every-round damage, the opponent's name — is read from the event log; the loose copies are gone and survive only as views.** Verified: suite 1731/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0; bot-only series pb5 (3 matches): handshake 124/124, names 122/122, steal 23 / 25, and the paths the change touches all fired — Taste of Vitae 4, Carrion Crows 2, the wounded rule ended 3 combats ('opp-down'), combat ends 9 each way; invariant flags 13 (strike-vs-taken against Fortitude vampires and pool −1 around Vessel — the known residue, tracked for the invariant pass, not phase-B regressions). The version line trimmed again (82c).
- **v0.6.183 (26 Sep 2026)** — PHASE B, step 3: the opponent's PRESS and MANEUVER live in the event log too — the same pending / consumed / replaced / dropped marks as the strike (v0.6.181), through one generic pair of helpers (`_pendingOf`, `_markPendingOf`). The narration parser now knows its speaker (`_narrWho`) for the marks; a line that reaches the parser without passing the recorder (a direct call, as in test 79b) is recorded there as a fallback event. `_oppPress` / `_oppManeuver` remain views (read) and a way to add one (write). The lines-only guardrail 127m exercises the real press and maneuver paths end to end. Verified: suite 1731/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0; bot-only series pb4 (3 matches): handshake 96/96, names 96/96, steal 22 / 17 (empty victims), range disagreement 2 (within earlier series' 0–2), invariant flags 8 (the known strike-vs-taken residue, 2 pool −1).
- **v0.6.182 (26 Sep 2026)** — PHASE B, step 2: the combat opponent's NAME lives in ONE combat episode (`this._cep`), opened at `_resolveCombat` entry and closed in its `finally`. The three loose fields — `_combatOppName` (live name), `_rowOpp` (the name the round rows keep after a later announce clears the live one, v0.6.180) and `_learnedOpp` (a name learned while armed, before the combat starts, v0.6.163) — with their separate resets are gone; they remain as views of the episode so the suite's 27 uses keep their shape. Every name the episode takes is a `combat-opp` event in the log (start / learned / armed / end). Caught by the suite while building: a trailing `//` comment swallowed the rest of a line (the damage accumulator's reset and the v0.6.168 field baseline) — 71b and 127o turned red at once. Also: 'Combat ends — Majesty.' names no vampire, so the strike-vs-taken check never saw it and kept flagging a Ventrue that ended combat (Oshri Dahan); it now counts from the opponent's seat. Caught a second time: the same trailing-`//` slip in that fix (a syntax error this time) — block comments from now on when a statement follows on the line. Verified on the final bytes: suite 1731/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0; bot-only series pb3 (3 matches): handshake 80/80, names 80/80, steal 13 / 13, invariant flags 5 (3 strike-vs-taken, 2 pool −1).
- **v0.6.181 (26 Sep 2026)** — PHASE B, step 1: the opponent's STRIKE lives in the event log (design Part 1). The loose `_oppStrike` copy is gone: when the combat accepts a strike line, its event (already recorded at the top of `_onLog`) is marked `stk: 'pending'`; `_awaitOppStrike` consumes it in the log (`'consumed'`); the latest unconsumed line wins as a correction (`'replaced'`), as the old stash did; a new combat drops the rest (`'dropped'`). `_oppStrike` survives as a VIEW of the first pending strike (read) and a way to add one (write), so the suite's 26 and the canary's 8 uses keep their shape without a second copy of the state. Verified: suite 1731/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0; bot-only series pb1 (3 matches): handshake 148/148, names 134/134, dealt==taken 129 of 134, steal 23 / 22, invariant flags 6 (5 strike-vs-taken, the checker's known nuance around crows and one Ventrue's prevention — tracked, not a phase-B regression). Next step: the combat opponent's name (`_combatOppName`, `_learnedOpp`, `_rowOpp`) moves to a combat episode read from the log.
- **v0.6.180 (26 Sep 2026)** — the invariant flags traced to their source: about 28 flags per match (v0.6.179's first run) down to **1 in 3 matches** (serie-inv16; handshake 106/106, names 98/98). **Every traced cause was a gap in the checker or the typing — the bot's own pool bookkeeping was right in every case.** *Strike vs taken:* their dodge / combat ends were not counted; the bot's own prevent line ("X plays CARD: prevents N damage.") was typed as card-played; an equipment prevent ("equipment prevents N", a note from the opponent's seat, no vampire named) was not counted; the check compared against the same vampire's NEXT combat — it now runs only while this combat does. *Pool ledger:* "I use 4 transfers to draw a new vampire" was read as 4 pool (a crypt draw is 4 transfers + 1 pool); a note's speaker prefix is stripped for any seat, not only mine; newly typed: Wider View-class burn-for-pool, a referendum's blood-or-pool split, Ashur Tablets, "X: +N pool", "locks X for +N pool", Smiling Jack's tick, Fame, a rescue's pool cost, pool → blood. *Silent pool changes* (no table line) now enter the ledger from the code: a political action's pool cost at announce, an ally recruit's pool cost, a blocked action's returned parked cost, my own referendum's swing — found with the new dev-only tracer `ELY_POOL_TRACE=1` (every change of my pool with its caller). **Open question for Johan:** those four silent changes are invisible to a human at the table too — should the bot print a line for each? Tests 127v +1 (the crypt draw). No play changed. Suite 1731/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.179 (26 Sep 2026)** — REAL-TIME INVARIANTS, observe only (design Part 3), read from the event log as it grows; a mismatch writes an `inv-flag` trace row (new tool `ana-inv.py`), nothing changes at the table. *(1) My pool ledger:* every change of my pool must be explained by a pool event of mine — checked at each turn and at every printed pool value. *(2) Strike vs taken:* my opponent's own taken line must equal my strike of that round (pinned from my own strike event, not from a field that may already belong to the next round) plus my Carrion Crows, minus what they prevented. **The first runs mostly found the CHECKER's own gaps, now fixed:** my line about another seat's pool (my bleed going through) counted as mine; a multi-part influence sentence counted only its first number; superior Govern the Unaligned ADDS bank blood ("Add 3 blood …"), it is not a pool cost — the bot was right, the typing wrong; a crypt draw costs 4 transfers + 1 pool (rulebook) — the bot was right, the ledger missed the pool; the note's speaker prefix hid Smiling Jack / Fame / rescue / pool→blood lines (now typed). Flags per match fell from about 28 to about 5. **Open (to investigate next):** pool drifts of +1…+5 on turns with bleeds taken (the ledger lower than the bot's pool by about the bleeds' sum — either a typing gap or a real bookkeeping difference), and strike-taken mismatches about once per match (taken 0 against a strike of 1 at close range). Tests 127v (3). Suite 1730/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0; series inv6 (3 matches): handshake 80/80, names 76/76.
- **v0.6.178 (26 Sep 2026)** — the event-log review. *(1) Entities:* the client's `escapeHtml` sends `&`, `<`, `>` as `&amp;` / `&lt;` / `&gt;`; `stripTags` never decoded them, so a card or vampire name with `&` read as `&amp;` in EVERY parser (not just the event log's card lookups). `stripTags` now decodes them (plus `&quot;` / `&#39;`), `&amp;` last. *(2) Tally events* record only the fields that CHANGED — every relay carries the whole state, so "fields present" said nothing — and no event when nothing changed. Reviewed and left as is: own lines recorded once (at send; echoes skipped), the classifier's order (strike shapes before bleed / card shapes), the dev dump guarded for the browser bundle, `EventLog` not clashing with any client identifier. Tests 127u +2. Suite 1727/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.177 (26 Sep 2026)** — the event log's second stage: the table's BOOKKEEPING lines are typed, so the conservation invariants (design Part 3) can read pool and blood moves from the log. New kinds: `phase`, `turn-passed`, `blocked`, `pool` (bleed result, cost paid with the card, the Edge, the oust bounty, Blood Doll, Creeping Sabotage, referendum — each with delta, new pool where printed, and cause), `blood` (hunt, Hunting Grounds, grants), `bleed-mod` (both the log and the ask forms), `influence`, `vote`, `ref-terms`, `ref-result`, `oust`, `discard`, `unlock`, `combat-start`, `card-burned`, `ask`, `note`; `card-played` also reads "plays X on Y". Measured with a dev-only dump of untyped lines (env `ELY_EV_DUMP=file`): untyped lines per match fell from about 1,700 to about 60 (96 % typed; the rest are notes and strength read-outs). Tests 127u +2. Suite 1725/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.176 (26 Sep 2026)** — THE EVENT LOG, phase A (design doc Part 1: observe only). `EventLog` (`this.ev`): every log / chat line — mine at `send`, theirs at receipt — every tally change and every turn becomes one typed event through ONE classifier (`_evClassify`): announce (action, directed / undirected), attempt, stealth, intercept (incl. the paid Bowl line), verdict, strike (hand / card / damage / aggravated / steal / dodge / ends / none), maneuver, range-set, press, extra-strike, env, taken (n, lost, now, down), prevent, card-played, resolves, tally (the fields that came), turn, other. The ring keeps the last 4,000 events; cards SHOWN per seat (`ev.cardsShown(seat)`) are kept for the whole match — the knowledge layer's first feed. No decision reads the log yet (phase B switches readers, combat first). One `ev-summary` trace row per turn. Regression (3 matches): handshake 106/106, names 102/102, bleed and steal ledgers balance; 3 matches produced 444 turn rows and typed 5,700+ events — the largest untyped share ('other', about 40 % of lines: pool / blood moves, draws, unlocks, notes) is the next phase's typing work, needed for the conservation invariants. Tests 127u (6). The long version line trimmed (82c). Suite 1723/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **cardfx compiler v1.9.1 (25 Sep 2026, bot unchanged at v0.6.175)** — the sweep's pass 2 (`sweep-pass2.py`: costs, requirements, crypt basics against KRCG). Curated library entries now INHERIT the data's discipline and clan requirements when they do not restate them (costs already did): 13 entries regained one (11 disciplines — Deep Song, Guard Dogs, Murder of Crows, Cats' Guidance, Magic of the Smith, Preternatural Strength, Revelations, Spying Mission, Underbridge Stray, Enticement, Foreshadowing Destruction — and 2 clans, Creeping Sabotage / Forced Confessional). Masters never inherit a clan (a clan icon on a master is not a requirement). Everything else agreed: 977 costs, all 1,785 crypt entries (capacity, clan, disciplines, group, title). test-cardfx +2 (261/0); suite 1717/0, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0; bot-only regression (3 matches) handshake 156/156, names 148/148.
- **v0.6.175 (25 Sep 2026)** — THE CARDFX SWEEP: RANGED (`REPORT-cardfx-sweep-full.md`, `sweep-full.py`: all 1,670 cardfx library entries / 2,396 modes against KRCG text with "As above" resolved). *cardfx:* compiler **v1.9.0** tags `ranged` on a strike / steal / env-damage mode from VTES's own marker (the R suffix, "Strike, ranged") — positive evidence only, never on a mode alone (109 modes; Stunt Cycle via curated). Two wrong values fixed in the curated tier: **Legion** `actStealth` 2 → 1 (the auto tier's max() took the +2 of the fledgling ability it grants), **Madman's Quill** loses a spurious `dem` bleed mode built from the attached card's passive. *Bot:* at LONG range only ranged card strikes, ranged steals and ranged weapon strikes are offered — 14 melee cards (Talbot's Chainsaw, Burning Touch, Putrefaction, Song in the Dark …) were usable at long before. Tests 127t (5), test-cardfx +3 (ranged pins, ranged never alone, curated pin 99). Suite 1717/0 ×3, test-cardfx 259/0, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.174 (25 Sep 2026)** — the cardfx sweep pilot's two bot fixes (`REPORT-cardfx-sweep-pilot.md`: 90 deck cards checked against KRCG `vtes.json` and vtes-rulings). *(1) Bowl of Convergence [AUS]:* the bearer can burn 1 blood during an action for an ADDITIONAL +1 intercept. cardfx: `interceptPaid` 1 at `interceptPaidBlood` 1 on the [AUS] mode (curated entry, recompiled from `vtes.json` — the only library entry that changed; curated count 97); bot: `_equipPaidIntercept`, used by the blocker's second look BEFORE a card and only while stealth beats intercept, once per action, keeping 1 blood. *(2) The Immortal Grapple ruling [TOM 19951217]:* a minion committed to a non-hand strike by its gun's maneuver (the .44 Magnum's) gets NO strike that round under Grapple — not a hand strike, no dodge, no combat ends (`_rnd.gunCommitted`, `_noStrikeRound`; the counter shows "No strike"). Also: the pilot corrected our stealth rule's REASON — an action card is at 0 stealth unless it prints stealth (rulebook, Action cards), so `actStealth` is the printed total; the undirected +1 is for basic actions only (CLAUDE.md block gate reworded). Tests 127s (3), test-cardfx +1 (Bowl's [AUS] fields) and its curated pin 96 → 97. Suite 1712/0 ×3, test-cardfx 256/0, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.173 (25 Sep 2026)** — STEALTH BY CHAT (Johan: "can one use the chat for blocks too, typing +1?"). The mirror of v0.6.152's intercept by chat: while the bot blocks a HUMAN's action (pending contested), the actor typing a bare number N (their total stealth now) or +N (stealth added; "stealth" optional, the @Name form accepted) is booked on the block counter's `a` and answered by the bot's intercept second look. A number from anyone else is table talk. With v0.6.152 both directions of the block exchange now work from chat alone; the client's general ("free") counter carries no block meaning to the bot and is deliberately not read. Test 127r (3). Suite 1709/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.172 (25 Sep 2026)** — main's REPLY (client v2.6.138 / srv-2.6.42). *(1) The bot closes its tallies:* `mode: null` on the block counter when its own action resolves (hooked in `_emit('through')` — every such site is followed by "It resolves") and on the combat tally when its combat is over (`_resolveCombat`'s finally). With main's own closes (a human's new action, every turn change server-side) the mirror is clean at once; the v0.6.168 stale-field guard stays as belt-and-braces within a turn. *(2) Undirected lines:* `takes an undirected action.` (the client's new "Undirected card action" row) and `attempts to rescue their own vampire from torpor.` open the predator/prey window; `attempts to rescue a vampire from torpor.` is DIRECTED since v2.6.138 (no window); the bot's own rescue writes the new "their own vampire" line. *(3) The bot's steal (main's option 1):* ONE line `NAME strikes: steals N blood.` with the strike label `Steal N` (the human's panel shows "Stolen: N blood — move it"; the client never moves blood); a dodge or a first-pair combat ends undoes the steal (the stolen blood is taken back). Tests: 37f updated (the one-line steal; the overflow case now uses a strike for 0, a dodge would undo the steal), 101g (own / directed rescue, undirected card action), 127q (3). `ana-ledger.py`: back-to-back combats within 4 s no longer read as wrong names (mutual pairs first). Suite 1706/0 ×3, canary 57/0 vs v2.6.138, hotseat 10/0. Regression serie-rep1 (bot-only, 5 matches): handshake 180/180, names 166/166 right, bleed and steal ledgers hold, `ana-block.py` no rule violations.
- **v0.6.171 (24 Sep 2026)** — STALE STEALTH (Johan's pre-handover review). A bot actor's block tally is closed only by a mode change, so an action that went through left its stealth on the counter; the next HUMAN action (a 0-stealth declared bleed, the human never opens the counter as actor) read it as its own — the blocker spent intercept it did not need (against v0.6.170's rule) and the verdict line showed the wrong numbers. A human actor's `a` now counts only if it changed after their announce (`_obs.at`), or was written by that human within 3 s before it; otherwise the bot seeds the rulebook default on the counter (`a` = 0 directed / 1 undirected) before its block — which also gives a human's undirected action its +1 on the counter. Bot actors unchanged (they always re-announce a fresh `a`). Test 127p extended. Suite 1700/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.170 (24 Sep 2026)** — NO STEALTH OR INTERCEPT THAT IS NOT NEEDED, bot-vs-bot too (Johan: the base rules forbid it). v0.6.169's intercept-only-when-needed rule in `_commitBlock` now applies to every actor: a block attempt spends an intercept card only when the stealth standing (0 directed, 1 undirected, or the tally's `a`) beats the blocker's free intercept (equipment + crypt); the actor answers with stealth (`_stealthSecondLook`), the blocker with intercept (`_interceptSecondLook`). The actor side was already stealth-only-when-needed (`ana-block.py`: 0 stealth plays without a block attempt, 0 looks answering when already ahead). Tests reworked for the stealth-first exchange: bot-vs-bot 36 (the defender blocks a 0-stealth Computer Hacking with no card, "Stealth 0 vs Intercept 0 — block succeeds"), the ladder's exact-number race guard becomes a CONSISTENCY guard (every verdict line must agree with its own numbers), 79g, 127p. Suite 1699/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0. Series `serie-blk1` (3 of 6 matches completed — the chain died at a tool timeout): `ana-block.py` 0 rule violations in 255 actions; blocks without an intercept card 47 / 69 (cmb15: 83 / 170); `ana-ledger.py` names 140/140, bleed pairing holds. New tool `ana-block.py` (stealth / intercept / block audit against the base rules). Johan's ruling recorded: superior Govern the Unaligned's announced stealth 1 is right (the undirected +1, no printed stealth).
- **v0.6.169 (23 Sep 2026)** — two pre-test fixes (Johan). *(1) Intercept only when needed, against a HUMAN actor:* the blocker spent an intercept card at once on every block — Johan saw it against his own declared bleed, which carries 0 stealth. `_commitBlock` now plays an intercept card only when the stealth standing at the attempt (0 directed, 1 undirected, or the tally's `a`) beats the blocker's free intercept (equipment + crypt); a later stealth raise is answered by `_interceptSecondLook` (v0.6.143d). Bot-vs-bot keeps the commit-first exchange until a series proves the second-look exchange there (the bot-vs-bot tests and the ladder assert are built around it). The old hotseat test 14b now shows 2 stealth before the bleed. *(2) Additional strikes do not leak:* after `NAME gets an additional strike.` the next strike line(s) that round are logged (`combat-extra-strike` row) and dropped — they were stashed and read as the NEXT round's strike. Additional strike pairs are still not resolved. Tests 127p (6 asserts). Suite 1699/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.168 (23 Sep 2026)** — STALE COMBAT FIELDS (Johan's pre-test review). The server keeps absent fields while the mode stays `combat` (v2.6.17 rule) and every relay carries the whole state, so a `ce`=1 / `rl`=1 / `pr` / `rng` left by an EARLIER combat could read as "combat ended", "range set" or "they pressed" in the next one — a false combat-ends would have skipped the bot's strike entirely (v0.6.167's bug-B guard). `_cField(k)` honours a field only if it changed since this combat began (baseline at `_resolveCombat` entry; for the per-round `ce` / `pr` / `rl` since the round's `rd` write). Test 127o. Suite 1693/0 ×3, canary 57/0, hotseat 10/0.
- **v0.6.167 (23 Sep 2026)** — THE EFFECT LINES (main's `HANDOFF-to-bot-2026-09-23-v2_6_134.md`, client v2.6.136 / srv-2.6.41, after Johan's first hotseat test). *Parsed:* `strikes for N.`, `strikes for N aggravated damage.`, `strikes: steals N blood.` (one line — the victim loses the blood), `strikes: combat ends.` (one line), `inflicts N damage on the opposing minion each round.` / `inflicts no more damage each round.`, and ` and is burned` on a taken line (down, case-insensitive). *Fixed (main's scripted run against the real v0.6.166):* **bug A** — I struck first, the human ended combat: my strike never lands (combat ends resolves first), no `dealt` narration, no Carrion Crows that round (no strike resolution, VEKN ruling via main), no `ph` writes below 6, straight to End of round; **bug B** — the human acted and ended combat first: the bot does not strike at all. *Rules from main's client (v2.6.135–136):* a human's dodge → my strike reaches nothing (narration `— dodged, 0`, my side of the counter 0); aggravated damage per VEKN 4 (prevention goes on it first; the first unprevented point wounds without costing blood unless already wounded; each further point costs 1 blood or the vampire is burned); a wounded combatant ends the combat — no press, no round 2 when either side's taken line says torpor / burned. My Carrion Crows writes `ea`/`eb`. *Found while building:* a human's strike line never answered "What is your strike?" — the strike parser returned before the phase nudge, so the step ran out its clock (likely part of the slowness in Johan's test). The phased combat no longer ends its narration with "tell me if your side differs". Canary section 4 rewritten for the effect-only `CMB_LINE` builders (**57/0** vs v2.6.136). Tests 127n (9 asserts). Suite 1692/0 ×3, hotseat gate 10/0. Not yet: writing `g` for my own aggravated strikes (no aggravated strikes in the four starter lists), additional strike pairs.
- **v0.6.166 (23 Sep 2026)** — the Damage step for a human (Johan's pre-client-test review). Main's client writes a human's automatic taken line only when the table steps PAST Damage resolution; the bot never moved `ph` off Strike, so Taste of Vitae and the torpor-shot press waited for a line that could not come (they fell back to the clock). `_phaseDamage()`: `ph` 4 opens the step (the human may prevent), the wait ends on their taken / prevent line, a pass, `ph` > 4 or the clock, then `ph` 5 steps past it. No question is chatted (Johan's list has none for damage). Test 127l extended. Suite 1683/0 ×3, canary 52/0, hotseat 10/0. Known limits for the client test: one exchange per step (after the bot plays in answer to a human, the human's further answer is not awaited); a silent human costs up to `phaseSecs` per step; no *Any additional strikes?* yet.
- **v0.6.165 (23 Sep 2026)** — THE COMBAT PHASES (Johan, 22 Sep: the bot drives the seven combat steps against a human; 23 Sep: Structured + helpers first, but built so a Classic table stays possible). Knob `combatPhases` (default 1; 0 = v0.6.164). Against a HUMAN combat opponent only (the opponent vampire's seat is a non-bot player) — bot-vs-bot is untouched (serie-cmb16 check: 0 phase rows, 72/72 opponent names right). Per step the acting side plays first: human acting → the bot asks *Anything before range? · Any maneuvers? · Anything before strikes? · What is your strike? · Any presses? · Anything before ending combat?* and waits, then plays its own card; bot acting → it plays, or says *I have nothing before range. · I have no maneuver. · I have nothing before strikes. · I have no press. · I have nothing before ending combat.*, then waits for the human. Damage resolution has no question (the taken lines carry it). The wait (`_phaseStep` / `_phaseWait`) ends on the human's combat line for the step, a quick phrase in chat (No / Pass / Nothing / Go on / Done …) or the quick-phrase buttons (No, Pass, No reaction, It resolves), a human moving `ph` past the step, or the clock (`phaseSecs`, default max(6, 3 × askSecs), restarted by any other activity of that human). Built on LINES — `ph` is only WRITTEN as a hint for the helper panel (main §6). Each step leaves a `combat-phase` trace row (step, when, acting, answer, ms). Not yet: "Any additional strikes?" (the bot resolves one strike pair per round). **Guardrail (127m):** a whole round against a human from LINES ONLY — no tally, helper off — must resolve (strike, range, press from the lines); any future field dependency turns it red. Tests 127l (9 asserts) + 127m. Suite 1683/0 ×3, canary 52/0 vs v2.6.132, hotseat gate 10/0.
- **v0.6.164 (23 Sep 2026)** — THE COMBAT FIELDS (main's `spec-bot-combat-fields-2026-09-23.md`, client **v2.6.132** / srv-2.6.39). *(0, main's pre-deploy finding)* my strike's damage is written on MY side of the combat counter (`a` acting / `b` opposing, side fixed at `_resolveCombat` entry from `_actingVamp`) with the strike label (`sa`/`sb`) — a human's Damage step read "Incoming 0" against a bot, and the client's automatic taken line never fired; the combat announce no longer sends `a:0, b:0` (the server starts fresh on the mode change; a late announce wiped a human's entered damage). *(1)* Fields as data, lines as the contract (main §6): `rng` written after my range step; a HUMAN's `rng` that disagrees with the last line wins (their correction); `rl`=1 or `NAME sets range to X.` skips the maneuver step; `sets range to X next round.` carries; `pr` written at my press (continue / end) and read when a human switched it without a line; `ce`=1 read as the opponent's combat ends; `pa`/`pb` written when I prevent; `rd` written for round 2+ (a changed rd starts a fresh round server-side). *(2)* New lines parsed: `sets range to close|long[ next round].` and `gets an / N additional strike(s)[ (CARD)].` (logged only — the bot still resolves one strike pair per round). *(3)* My superior Immortal Grapple emits `NAME sets range to close next round.` + `nx`. *(4)* A human's `maneuvers to close.` at close no longer flips the range (direction, not parity). Canary section 4: the client's `CMB_LINE` builders extracted from v2.6.132 and every line fed through a live bot (15 asserts) — **52/0**. Tests 127k (5 asserts). Suite 1673/0 ×3, hotseat gate 10/0 vs v2.6.132. `START-HERE-BOTS.html`: the `Combat ▸` menu and `Damage taken…` described for players (main §6.5). *(5) The null-opponent rows explained:* a diagnostic on every round row (`strikeSeen`, `rejects` — every combat line the name filter refused) showed 0 refused lines in 252 round-1 rows of serie-cmb14, and every row without an opponent name HAD received the strike — the actor's next announce clears the live name (`_clearCombatStashes`) before the finished combat's row is written. Statistics only; the row now keeps the name captured at combat start / learn time (`_rowOpp`). Not yet: the release of the helper lock (main §6.6 — after the bot proves a whole combat on lines alone), additional strike pairs, `combatPhases`.
- **v0.6.163 (23 Sep 2026)** — THE BUG SWEEP (Johan asked for a sweep while main builds the combat controls). Method: a two-sided LEDGER audit of the traces (`ana-ledger.py`, new) — everything one bot moves to another must show on both sides. Bleed (238 / 239 pool losses pair with the actor's bleed, the rest explained by the actor's modifiers), referendum pool damage and steal balance hold. Found and fixed: *(1)* `L12.attemptIcp` — the bot's own frozen `X attempts to block with +N intercept (CARD).` (unlock cards carrying intercept, Second Tradition, v0.6.139) was never parsed, so the actor kept the PREVIOUS attempt's vampire as its combat opponent (9 misnamed combats in cmb5–7, all Ventrue) — lines from the real opponent could be refused by the handshake filter. *(2)* A blocker whose pending carried no actor name (≈4 % of round-1 rows, mostly re-blocks after Guard Dogs / On the Qui Vive) takes it from the short-term action memory `_obs`; and `_learnOpp` names an unknown opponent from the first combat line naming another vampire. *(3)* After the opponent's combat ends (Majesty) my row records dealt 0 (it said 1). *(4)* Creeping Sabotage's victim leaves a `took-pooldmg` row (`via: 'creep'`) — the burn always happened, the ledger could not see it. *(5) THE REFUSED STRIKE (the real cause behind the null opponents, found in serie-cmb11):* in a combat with no confirmed opponent, `_expectedOppVamp` could still return a STALE block-attempt name (`_lastBlockerName` via a leftover `_actingVamp`), and `_stashAccept` REFUSED the true opponent's strike line — the round fell back to the rulebook default (1, or 0 at long) in ~4–5 % of combats, in every series since v0.6.103. Now, in combat without a confirmed `_combatOppName`, any other vampire's combat line is accepted and names the opponent (only two minions fight at a time); the received strike names them too. Tests 127j (8 asserts), 46e adjusted (a new combat resets the learned opponent). Suite 1668/0 ×3 (one arena-test flake under series CPU load, green on re-run), canary 37/0, hotseat gate 10/0. Series `serie-cmb8` … `serie-cmb12` read with `ana-ledger.py`.
- **v0.6.162 (23 Sep 2026)** — RULES OUT OF THE KNOB (Johan's pre-spec code review). `combatMind` gated RULES corrections together with Johan's decisions, so the documented rollback (`combatMind: 0`) would have re-introduced rule bugs. Unconditional now: hand strikes not offered / dealing 0 at long range (`_bestStrikeFor`, `longFist`, `_myRoundDmg`), a silent opponent at long deals 0 unless a ranged option is shown, a continued round waits two strike windows (`_strikeWaitMs`, the race), the victim of a steal loses the blood, and a prevent-bundled press (Hidden Strength [FOR] — a damage-resolution card) is never a standalone press. `combatMind` gates DECISIONS only: Grapple gating, bundled-press arming/use, press worth / press-to-end, range posture, Taste threshold, stealth order, rush kit, seize the moment, chase press. Tests 126a / 127f reworded, 127i (3 asserts: the rules survive `combatMind: 0`). Suite 1661/0 ×3, canary 37/0, hotseat gate 10/0. No series (behaviour at `combatMind: 1` is byte-for-byte the same decisions).
- **v0.6.161 (23 Sep 2026)** — THE CHASE PRESS (Johan, after comparing the bots with Black Chantry's V5 strategy guides: "fix it so it presses via the Stray, definitely"). The Nosferatu guide: when an opposing minion maneuvers to long, burn 1 life from an Underbridge Stray in the press step and continue. `_wantPress` for a chase-posture deck: the round was fought at long (a chase deck never goes long itself, so they waved) and I survive their shown return → press (tag `chase-wave`), consumed by any press available — for the V5 Nosferatu the Stray's life. combatMind. Test 127h. Suite 1658/0 ×3, canary 37/0, hotseat gate 10/0. Series `serie-cmb7` vs `serie-cmb6`. **Johan's rulings in the same exchange (not built, deferred):** the block doctrine's "do not block Nosferatu / Tremere unless you must" and a dynamic Tremere Apportation choice (maneuver when combat is expected from the opponent, press against pacifist decks) wait for the knowledge / meta layer, where opponent-deck expectations are general rather than hard-coded; Nosferatu's Grapple against combat-ends decks is already covered by the damage-gated rule where it matters most.
- **v0.6.160 (22 Sep 2026)** — THE VICTIM OF A STEAL (Johan asked how steals are counted in the stats). The victim side of Steal Blood was never built: since v0.6.19 the stealing bot gained the blood and its frozen strike line correctly said "for 0" (a steal is not damage), but the opposing bot read that 0 and lost NOTHING — every Theft of Vitae against a bot created blood from nowhere. `_parseCombatNarration` now reads the stealer's note ("X strikes with CARD: steals N blood") from my combat opponent and takes min(N, blood) from `_combatMyVamp` (set in `_resolveCombat`); not damage, so a steal to 0 is not a torpor. The `combat-round` row carries `stole` (mine) and `stolenFromMe`; `ana-combat-sum.py` prints them as their own columns (a steal is a blood swing, never counted in dealt). combatMind. Test 127g. Suite 1654/0 ×3, canary 37/0, hotseat gate 10/0. serie-cmb6 (seeds 9981–9992): Tremere stole 54 blood, the victims lost 50; Tremere 0/1 → 3/12 (W/VP), Malkavian 7/28 → 5/18; hunts Malkavian 25 → 37, Nosferatu 9 → 23, Ventrue 21 → 38.
- **v0.6.159 (22 Sep 2026)** — THE CONTINUED-ROUND RACE (found reading serie-cmb4's unmatched rounds, Johan asked for a complete combat read). After a press the receiver starts the next round at once while the presser still waits out its own press window; with equal windows the receiver's strike wait expired as the presser's strike arrived (one side "opp-silent", the other a lone extra round — twice in cmb4, Ventrue's bundled Hidden Strength press vs Malkavian). `_strikeWaitMs(continued)`: round 2+ waits two windows (combatMind; three `_awaitOppStrike` sites). Test 127f. `ana-combat2.py` match window 4 s → 9 s (a presser's row is written up to one window late). New `ana-combat-sum.py` (one line per deck per series). Suite 1651/0, canary 37/0, hotseat gate 10/0. serie-cmb5: handshake 342/343. `REPORT-combat-komplett.md` reads all six series.
- **v0.6.158 (22 Sep 2026)** — SEIZE THE MOMENT (Johan, after serie-cmb3: "a torpor-certain rush should trump permanents-first if it is likely to go through — combat is about seizing the moment when it presents itself"; behind `combatMind`). In `decide('action')`, before the permFirst gate: a rush is picked at **97** when it is torpor-certain — `expDmg > target blood` in one round, or Immortal Grapple behind a damage source with `reach > target blood` (the bundled press buys round 2) — and `_rushLikelyThrough(target)`: the target's controller has no OTHER unlocked ready vampire or ally (the rushed vampire itself blocking still gives the fight). Never with a rusher at 0 blood. The permFirst gate now requires `sPick < 97`; the 99+ structural picks (own rescue while building, referendum) stay above. `scores.rushSeize` carries the reason, the why reads "seize the moment". The BOT_VERSION line was trimmed (82c: no line over 4 kB) — the full notes live here. Section 127 (7 asserts). Suite 1649/0 ×3, canary 37/0, hotseat gate 10/0. Series `serie-cmb4` (seeds 9981–9992).
- **v0.6.157 (22 Sep 2026)** — THE RUSH KIT + THE SILENT LONG (Johan, before the deploy; both behind `combatMind`). *(1)* `_oppHasRanged()` (a strike-granting non-hand equipment on the opponent vampire, or a steal seen from the seat); in `_resolveCombatRound` a SILENT opponent at LONG range with no ranged option shown deals 0 (their rulebook default is a hand strike) — the combat mind sends Tremere / the wave decks to long far more often, and against a human who never types the strike the old assume-1 was wrong there. *(2)* `_rushExpectation` returns the kit: `grapple` (via the range-free `_bestImmortalGrappleFor2`), `strikeCard` (a 2+ damage card), `crows` (hand or borne), `dmgSource`, `kit`, and `reach` (= expDmg × 2 when Grapple stands behind a damage source — superior's bundled press / the Stray buys the second round). `decide('action')`'s rush score × kitW 1.5 / 1.15 / 0.75 and its torpor-reach multiplier reads `reach`; `rushParts` carries kit / kitW / reach, the why names the Grapple reach. `_rushScore` (target choice) reads the same reach, cached per targeting pass. Section 126 (11 asserts). Suite 1642/0 ×3, canary 37/0, hotseat gate 10/0. Series `serie-cmb3` (seeds 9981–9992) vs `serie-cmb2b`, read with new `ana-rush.py`.
- **v0.6.156 (22 Sep 2026)** — THE COMBAT MIND (Johan's combat rulings after `REPORT-serie-cmb1.md`; knob `combatMind`, 0 = v0.6.155). *(A) The handshake regression, found while building:* since v0.6.114 (16 Sep) the bot's combat EVENT lines (press / maneuver / Immortal Grapple / Carrion Crows) went to the log tier or were deleted as "duplicates", while the opposing bot's parsers ran only in `_onChat` — so for six days no press, maneuver, Grapple or Crows reached a bot opponent: a pressing bot fought round 2 alone (`opponentSilent`), Theft of Vitae stole blood from nobody, Grapple never restricted anyone, ranges never moved together. Fix: the four parsers live in `_parseCombatNarration(txt)`, fed from `_onChat` AND `_onLog` (strike lines were already on the log tier); the three silent sites announce their frozen phrase as a `note()` (`X maneuvers to long (CARD).`, `X presses to continue (CARD).`, the Grapple sentence). Verified: serie-cmb1b (this bot, `combatMind=0`) — 338 of 338 rounds have a matching row on the opponent's side. *(B) Johan's rules:* `_wantGrapple` — Immortal Grapple only with Roundhouse-class hand damage (≥2), active Carrion Crows or a strength bonus, never for a fist of 1; superior Grapple's optional press and Hidden Strength [FOR]'s (when played as a PREVENT — `_bestPreventFor` now prefers the press-bundled mode on ties) are CONSUMED via the per-round `_bundledPress`; `_wantPress(vamp, have)` replaces `blood > 1`: torpor shot (my next-round damage beats their blood and I survive their shown return), a card round (Roundhouse / active Crows / a steal for snipe decks), never into an opponent whose own taken line says torpor or burn (`_oppTaken.down`); wave decks press only for the torpor shot; press-to-END with a press card or bundled press cancels an incoming press (`_wantPressToEnd`: wave always, chase/snipe when the round would cost more than it earns), and their `presses to end` cancels mine; `_bestPressFor` excludes prevent-bundled modes; Taste of Vitae only after the opponent lost 2+; `DECK_STRATEGY.combat` = chase (nosferatu) / snipe (tremere, wall) / wave (the rest) read by `_wantLong` in the range step (snipe goes long with a ranged strike/steal in hand, wave with no strike card against a dangerous seat, chase never); at LONG range hand strikes deal 0 — Roundhouse-class cards are not offered and the fist announces `strikes with hands for 0` on the frozen strikeC channel; a steal at long is taken even at full capacity (it drains the opponent); `_bestStealthFor` prefers a pure stealth card over a dual stealth/combat card (Swallowed by the Night) at equal stealth. *(C) The trace:* one `combat-round` row per round (round, vamp, opp, range, strike, dealt, taken, grapple, press tag, continuing, end reason, posture) — `ana-combat2.py` reads it and computes handshake integrity. Sections 37h/i/k/n keep the old thresholds under `combatMind: 0`; section 125 (30 asserts) covers the new rules and the parsers on the log tier. Suite 1631/0 ×3, canary 37/0 vs client v2.6.125, hotseat gate 10/0. Arena **v1.4.3** whitelists `combatMind`. Series: `serie-cmb1` (v0.6.155) · `serie-cmb1b` (`combatMind=0`) · `serie-cmb2` (final bytes), seeds 9981–9992 — `REPORT-serie-cmb2.md`.
